# T08H-P1 clearance feasibility diagnostics

Full opening-offseason sequence; no season advancement. Each snapshot reports current state only.
“Legal at 400” uses the authoritative evaluator; willingness runs player response separately. Hypothetical refusal is never counted when finance rejects the offer. Recorded rejections are formal attempts. Valuation uses public OVR, age and position only.

## Owner decision comparison

- **A — preserve 400 CU minimum-only relief:** no economic-rule change; remaining franchises stay blocked where player policy refuses legal 400 CU offers.
- **B — individually recorded hardship exceptions:** each post-clearance team section reports cheapest acceptable salary, exact extra cap needed, cash projection and future commitments. Apply only one contract per vacancy; no loan or forced acceptance.
- **C — unsigned rookie rights:** a no-change signing trial fills a user-controlled vacancy in 3 of 12 runs. AI rights have persisted deferredLowFit decisions, so they cannot fill AI vacancies without a modeled decision change.

**Recommendation: Option B for owner approval.** AI clearance blockers have no affordable willing free agent under current relief; Option C only clears tested user-controlled vacancies. No player in blocked-team tables accepts a financially legal 400 CU hypothetical offer. Option B uses evidenced thresholds and per-team shortfalls; it does not guarantee competitive wins. It remains unimplemented pending owner approval.
**Final clearance: NO-GO.** Remaining standard-roster vacancies remain after the one authorized pass.
